001993:     private static bool RangeContainsLabelV734(

001906:     private static object CanonicalMemory(object memory)

001909:         {

001993:     private static bool RangeContainsLabelV734(
001994:         ulong start,
001995:         ulong length,
001996:         ulong label)
001997:     {
001998:         if (start == 0 || length == 0)
001999:         {
002000:             return false;
002001:         }
002002: 
002003:         var end = start > ulong.MaxValue - length
002004:             ? ulong.MaxValue
002005:             : start + length;
002006:         return label >= start && label < end;
002007:     }

018502:         // SHARPEMU_V74_0_4_DCC_NO_CPU_SNAPSHOT
018503:         //
018504:         // Producer/resident/upload-known cases have already returned above.
018505:         // What remains here is a metadata-backed sampled surface with no
018506:         // trustworthy Vulkan producer. DCC bytes are compressed metadata/
018507:         // color blocks, not a linear/tiled texture upload. The old V73.17
018508:         // path scanned the whole range and suppressed only when every byte
018509:         // was zero. A non-zero 4K DCC range therefore allocated tens of MiB
018510:         // per draw and uploaded semantically invalid pixels.

018512:         // For large DCC surfaces, never snapshot CPU bytes. A later real GPU
018513:         // writer/resident image re-enters one of the authoritative paths
018514:         // above. Keep the old zero-only guard for small metadata surfaces.

018517:             physicalSourceByteCount >= 8UL * 1024UL * 1024UL)
018518:         {

018520:             //
018521:             // V56.31 proves that these are not merely expensive snapshots:
018522:             // 3840x2160 and 2560x1440 scene inputs repeatedly reach this branch
018523:             // and the old code replaces each one with Address=0, 1x1 black.
018524:             //
018525:             // DCC bytes cannot be decoded as ordinary guest RAM here. Preserve

001687:     private const long V74015LargeArrayThresholdBytes = 64L * 1024L * 1024L;
001688: 
001689:     // V74.0.64: V74.0.15 used one global 320 MiB-class snapshot slot and a
001690:     // fixed 500 ms TTL. Demon's Souls alternates at least two large array
001691:     // identities before the Vulkan texture cache becomes visible, causing the
001692:     // slot to thrash and the producer queue to retain/copy 320-642 MiB again.
001693:     //
001694:     // Retain a very small bounded set of exact keys. Two entries cover the
001695:     // measured 1024x1024x80 and 4096x4096x5 arrays while capping the bridge
001696:     // cache to roughly 640 MiB for that workload. The value can be reduced to
001697:     // 1 for A/B or raised only as far as 4.
001698:     private static readonly int _v74064LargeArraySnapshotCacheEntries =
001699:         Math.Clamp(
001700:             int.TryParse(
001701:                 Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(
001702:                     "SHARPEMU_LARGE_ARRAY_SNAPSHOT_CACHE_ENTRIES"),
001703:                 out var v74064LargeArrayCacheEntries) &&
001704:             v74064LargeArrayCacheEntries > 0
001705:                 ? v74064LargeArrayCacheEntries
001706:                 : 2,
001707:             1,
001708:             4);
001709: 
001710:     // The downstream sampled-texture cache already treats an untracked cached
001711:     // identity as static when the CPU write tracker is disabled. This cache is
001712:     // only the producer->presenter bridge before that resource cache is
001713:     // visible, so use a longer bounded bridge interval instead of expiring in
001714:     // the middle of a multi-second UI frame.
001715:     private static readonly long _v74064LargeArraySnapshotTtlMs =
001716:         long.TryParse(
001717:             Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(
001718:                 "SHARPEMU_LARGE_ARRAY_SNAPSHOT_REUSE_MS"),
001719:             out var v74064LargeArrayReuseMs) &&
001720:         v74064LargeArrayReuseMs > 0
001721:             ? Math.Min(v74064LargeArrayReuseMs, 30000L)
001722:             : 10000L;
001723:     // V74.0.67.2.15 effective large-array TTL.
001724:     // V74.0.64 capped the environment-derived value at 30 seconds.
001725:     // Re-read the existing public knob here with a 120-second upper bound.
001726:     // The cache remains bounded by the existing entry-count limit.
001727:     private static long V74067215LargeArraySnapshotTtlMs
001728:     {
001729:         get
001730:         {

001732:                 "SHARPEMU_LARGE_ARRAY_SNAPSHOT_REUSE_MS");
001733:             if (long.TryParse(configured, out var requested))
001734:             {
001735:                 return Math.Clamp(requested, 1000L, 120000L);
001736:             }
001737: 
001738:             return Math.Clamp(_v74064LargeArraySnapshotTtlMs, 1000L, 120000L);
001739:         }
001740:     }

018501:                 // SHARPEMU_V73_17_UNRESOLVED_DCC_ZERO_SUPPRESSION
018502:         // SHARPEMU_V74_0_4_DCC_NO_CPU_SNAPSHOT
018503:         //
018504:         // Producer/resident/upload-known cases have already returned above.
018505:         // What remains here is a metadata-backed sampled surface with no
018506:         // trustworthy Vulkan producer. DCC bytes are compressed metadata/
018507:         // color blocks, not a linear/tiled texture upload. The old V73.17
018508:         // path scanned the whole range and suppressed only when every byte
018509:         // was zero. A non-zero 4K DCC range therefore allocated tens of MiB
018510:         // per draw and uploaded semantically invalid pixels.
018511:         //
018512:         // For large DCC surfaces, never snapshot CPU bytes. A later real GPU
018513:         // writer/resident image re-enters one of the authoritative paths
018514:         // above. Keep the old zero-only guard for small metadata surfaces.
018515:         if (!isStorage &&
018516:             descriptor.DccCompressionEnabled &&
018517:             physicalSourceByteCount >= 8UL * 1024UL * 1024UL)
018518:         {
018519:             // SHARPEMU_V74_0_56_32_DEFER_DCC_TO_GPU_METADATA
018520:             //
018521:             // V56.31 proves that these are not merely expensive snapshots:
018522:             // 3840x2160 and 2560x1440 scene inputs repeatedly reach this branch
018523:             // and the old code replaces each one with Address=0, 1x1 black.
018524:             //
018525:             // DCC bytes cannot be decoded as ordinary guest RAM here. Preserve

008546:     internal static int CompactCompletedEntries<T>(
008547:         List<T> entries,
008548:         Func<T, bool> isCompleted,
008549:         int targetCount)
008550:     {
008551:         ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(entries);
008552:         ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(isCompleted);
008553:         targetCount = Math.Max(0, targetCount);
008554: 
008555:         // Single order-preserving pass. Removing one-by-one would shift the
008556:         // tail on every eviction, which is quadratic on a list this size and
008557:         // runs while the label gate is held.
008558:         var removable = entries.Count - targetCount;
008559:         var removed = 0;
008560:         var write = 0;
008561:         for (var read = 0; read < entries.Count; read++)
008562:         {
008563:             if (removed < removable && isCompleted(entries[read]))
008564:             {
008565:                 removed++;
008566:                 continue;
008567:             }
008568: 
008569:             entries[write++] = entries[read];
008570:         }
008571: 
008572:         entries.RemoveRange(write, entries.Count - write);
008573:         return removed;
008574:     }

008833:     private static void NotePendingAcquireInvalidation(
008834:         SubmittedDcbState state,
008835:         ulong baseAddress,
008836:         ulong sizeBytes)
008837:     {
008838:         if (!state.PendingAcquireInvalidation)
008839:         {
008840:             state.PendingAcquireInvalidation = true;
008841:             state.PendingAcquireBase = baseAddress;
008842:             state.PendingAcquireSize = sizeBytes;
008843:             return;
008844:         }
008845: 
008846:         if (state.PendingAcquireSize == ulong.MaxValue || sizeBytes == ulong.MaxValue)
008847:         {
008848:             state.PendingAcquireBase = 0;
008849:             state.PendingAcquireSize = ulong.MaxValue;
008850:             return;
008851:         }
008852: 
008853:         var existingEnd = state.PendingAcquireBase > ulong.MaxValue - state.PendingAcquireSize
008854:             ? ulong.MaxValue
008855:             : state.PendingAcquireBase + state.PendingAcquireSize;
008856:         var newEnd = baseAddress > ulong.MaxValue - sizeBytes
008857:             ? ulong.MaxValue
008858:             : baseAddress + sizeBytes;
008859:         var mergedBase = Math.Min(state.PendingAcquireBase, baseAddress);
008860:         var mergedEnd = Math.Max(existingEnd, newEnd);
008861:         state.PendingAcquireBase = mergedBase;
008862:         state.PendingAcquireSize = mergedEnd == ulong.MaxValue
008863:             ? ulong.MaxValue
008864:             : mergedEnd - mergedBase;
008865:     }

007802:                 $"[V74.0.71][DEDICATED_WAIT_DRAIN] worker_start_failed " +

007808:     private static void RunDedicatedResumableDcbDrainWorkerV74071(
007809:         object? state)
007810:     {
007811:         if (state is not SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007812:         {
007813:             return;
007814:         }
007815: 
007816:         while (true)
007817:         {
007818:             gpuState.DedicatedDrainSignal.WaitOne();
007819: 
007820:             while (true)
007821:             {
007822:                 Interlocked.Exchange(ref gpuState.DrainPending, 0);
007823:                 var drainContext =
007824:                     Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.PendingDrainContext);
007825: 
007826:                 var resumed = 0;
007827:                 var gateWaitMilliseconds = 0.0;
007828: 
007829:                 if (drainContext is not null)
007830:                 {
007831:                     var gateWaitStart =
007832:                         System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
007833: 
007834:                     lock (gpuState.Gate)
007835:                     {
007836:                         gateWaitMilliseconds =
007837:                             (System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() -
007838:                              gateWaitStart) *
007839:                             1000.0 /
007840:                             System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.Frequency;
007841: 
007842:                         resumed = DrainResumableDcbs(
007843:                             drainContext,
007844:                             gpuState,
007845:                             tracePackets: _traceAgc);
007846:                     }
007847: 
007848:                     var traceCount = Interlocked.Increment(
007849:                         ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainTraceCount);
007850:                     var slowGate = gateWaitMilliseconds >= 5.0;
007851:                     if (slowGate)
007852:                     {
007853:                         Interlocked.Increment(
007854:                             ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainSlowGateCount);
007855:                     }
007856: 
007857:                     if (resumed > 0 ||
007858:                         slowGate ||
007859:                         traceCount <= 128 ||
007860:                         (traceCount & (traceCount - 1)) == 0)
007861:                     {
007862:                         Console.Error.WriteLine(
007863:                             $"[V74.0.71][DEDICATED_WAIT_DRAIN] " +
007864:                             $"count={traceCount} resumed={resumed} " +
007865:                             $"gate_wait_ms={gateWaitMilliseconds:F3} " +
007866:                             $"pending={Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending)} " +
007867:                             $"slow_gate={Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainSlowGateCount)}");
007868:                     }
007869:                 }
007870: 
007871:                 // Producer completion can race with the drain above. If it
007872:                 // marked another pass pending, consume it immediately on this
007873:                 // same dedicated worker rather than scheduling a new host job.
007874:                 if (Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending) != 0)
007875:                 {
007876:                     continue;
007877:                 }
007878: 
007879:                 break;
007880:             }
007881:         }
007882:     }
007883: 
007884:     // V74.0.72: V71 proved the dominant remaining producer->waiter latency is
007885:     // contention on SubmittedGpuState.Gate, not ThreadPool startup. This helper
007886:     // is deliberately narrower than V70's direct-drain experiment:
007887:     //
007888:     // - it runs only on a thread that ALREADY owns gpuState.Gate;
007889:     // - it is called only between fully parsed PM4 packets;
007890:     // - it never acquires Gate from the Vulkan presenter/completion thread;
007891:     // - it preserves/restores the outer DCB snapshot because a resumed queue
007892:     //   can recursively enter ParseSubmittedDcb;
007893:     // - GateOwnerDrainActive prevents recursive drain-on-drain.
007894:     //
007895:     // No guest label/value/fence is synthesized. The exact existing
007896:     // DrainResumableDcbs comparison/generation logic remains authoritative.
007897:     private static readonly bool _agcGateOwnerWaitDrainV74072 =
007898:         !string.Equals(
007899:             Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(
007900:                 "SHARPEMU_AGC_GATE_OWNER_WAIT_DRAIN"),
007901:             "0",
007902:             StringComparison.Ordinal);
007903: 
007904:     private static void TryDrainPendingWaitersOnGateOwnerV74072(
007905:         CpuContext ctx,
007906:         SubmittedGpuState gpuState,
007907:         SubmittedDcbState currentState,
007908:         ulong commandAddress,
007909:         uint packetOffset,
007910:         bool tracePackets)
007911:     {
007912:         if (!_agcGateOwnerWaitDrainV74072 ||
007913:             Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending) == 0 ||
007914:             !System.Threading.Monitor.IsEntered(gpuState.Gate) ||
007915:             Interlocked.CompareExchange(
007916:                 ref gpuState.GateOwnerDrainActiveV74072,
007917:                 1,
007918:                 0) != 0)
007919:         {
007920:             return;
007921:         }
007922: 
007923:         var previousBuffer = _dcbWindowBuffer;
007924:         var previousStart = _dcbWindowStart;
007925:         var previousLength = _dcbWindowByteLength;
007926:         var resumed = 0;
007927: 
007928:         try
007929:         {
007930:             // Consume the current producer-completion request. A completion
007931:             // racing with this drain can set DrainPending again; the next PM4
007932:             // packet boundary (or the V71 worker after Gate release) handles it.
007933:             Interlocked.Exchange(ref gpuState.DrainPending, 0);
007934: 
007935:             // WAIT labels are data addresses, not PM4 bytes. Force waiter
007936:             // comparisons to read canonical guest memory while nested resumed
007937:             // parsers are free to install their own DCB snapshots.
007938:             _dcbWindowBuffer = null;
007939:             _dcbWindowStart = 0;
007940:             _dcbWindowByteLength = 0;
007941: 
007942:             resumed = DrainResumableDcbs(
007943:                 ctx,
007944:                 gpuState,
007945:                 tracePackets: tracePackets || _traceAgc);
007946: 
007947:             if (resumed != 0)
007948:             {
007949:                 Interlocked.Add(
007950:                     ref gpuState.GateOwnerDrainResumeCountV74072,
007951:                     resumed);
007952:             }
007953: 
007954:             var traceCount = Interlocked.Increment(
007955:                 ref gpuState.GateOwnerDrainTraceCountV74072);
007956:             if (resumed != 0 ||
007957:                 traceCount <= 128 ||
007958:                 (traceCount & (traceCount - 1)) == 0)
007959:             {
007960:                 Console.Error.WriteLine(
007961:                     $"[V74.0.72][GATE_OWNER_WAIT_DRAIN] " +
007962:                     $"count={traceCount} resumed={resumed} " +
007963:                     $"resumed_total={Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.GateOwnerDrainResumeCountV74072)} " +
007964:                     $"queue={currentState.QueueName} " +
007965:                     $"submission={currentState.ActiveSubmissionId} " +
007966:                     $"cmd=0x{commandAddress:X16} dw={packetOffset} " +
007967:                     $"pending={Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending)}");
007968:             }
007969:         }
007970:         finally
007971:         {
007972:             _dcbWindowBuffer = previousBuffer;
007973:             _dcbWindowStart = previousStart;
007974:             _dcbWindowByteLength = previousLength;
007975:             Volatile.Write(ref gpuState.GateOwnerDrainActiveV74072, 0);
007976:         }
007977:     }

007979:     private static void RunResumableDcbDrainWorker(SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007980:     {
007981:         while (true)
007982:         {
007983:             Interlocked.Exchange(ref gpuState.DrainPending, 0);
007984:             if (Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.PendingDrainContext) is { } drainContext)
007985:             {
007986:                 lock (gpuState.Gate)
007987:                 {
007988:                     DrainResumableDcbs(drainContext, gpuState, tracePackets: _traceAgc);
007989:                 }
007990:             }
007991: 
007992:             if (Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending) != 0)
007993:             {
007994:                 continue;
007995:             }
007996: 
007997:             Volatile.Write(ref gpuState.DrainWorkerActive, 0);
007998:             if (Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending) == 0 ||
007999:                 Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref gpuState.DrainWorkerActive, 1, 0) != 0)
008000:             {
008001:                 return;
008002:             }
008003:         }
008004:     }

007885:     // contention on SubmittedGpuState.Gate, not ThreadPool startup. This helper

007979:     private static void RunResumableDcbDrainWorker(SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007980:     {

007982:         {

007993:             {

008025:     // remains enabled, but its PM4 reads now hit a thread-local byte window
008026:     // instead of taking the guest-memory reader path once per dword.
008027:     private static readonly bool _tracePm4PreindexBulkV74062 =
008028:         string.Equals(
008029:             Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SHARPEMU_TRACE_PM4_PREINDEX_BULK"),
008030:             "1",
008031:             StringComparison.Ordinal);
008032:     private static long _pm4PreindexBulkScanCountV74062;
008033:     private static long _pm4PreindexBulkDwordsV74062;
008034:     private static long _pm4PreindexBulkFallbackCountV74062;
008035: 
008036:     private static void PreindexSubmittedProducerIntentsCore(
008037:         CpuContext ctx,
008038:         SubmittedDcbState state,
008039:         ulong commandAddress,
008040:         uint dwordCount,
008041:         ulong submissionId,
008042:         HashSet<(ulong Address, uint Dwords)> visited,
008043:         int depth)
008044:     {
008045:         if (commandAddress == 0 ||
008046:             dwordCount == 0 ||
008047:             dwordCount > 1_000_000 ||
008048:             depth > MaxSubmittedChainDepth ||
008049:             !visited.Add((commandAddress, dwordCount)))
008050:         {
008051:             return;
008052:         }
008053: 
008054:         var byteCount = checked((int)(dwordCount * sizeof(uint)));
008055:         var rented = GuestDataPool.Shared.Rent(byteCount);
008056: 
008057:         // Preindex can recurse through INDIRECT_BUFFER. Preserve the parent
008058:         // thread-local DCB window while a child chain gets its own snapshot.
008059:         var previousBuffer = _dcbWindowBuffer;
008060:         var previousStart = _dcbWindowStart;
008061:         var previousLength = _dcbWindowByteLength;
008062:         var bulkLoaded = false;
008063: 
008064:         try
008065:         {
008066:             _dcbWindowBuffer = null;
008067:             _dcbWindowStart = 0;
008068:             _dcbWindowByteLength = 0;
008069: 
008070:             if (ctx.Memory.TryRead(commandAddress, rented.AsSpan(0, byteCount)))
008071:             {
008072:                 _dcbWindowBuffer = rented;
008073:                 _dcbWindowStart = commandAddress;
008074:                 _dcbWindowByteLength = byteCount;
008075:                 bulkLoaded = true;
008076:             }
008077:             else
008078:             {
008079:                 Interlocked.Increment(ref _pm4PreindexBulkFallbackCountV74062);
008080:             }
008081: 
008082:             var scanCount =
008083:                 Interlocked.Increment(ref _pm4PreindexBulkScanCountV74062);
008084:             var totalDwords =
008085:                 Interlocked.Add(ref _pm4PreindexBulkDwordsV74062, dwordCount);

007884:     // V74.0.72: V71 proved the dominant remaining producer->waiter latency is
007885:     // contention on SubmittedGpuState.Gate, not ThreadPool startup. This helper
007886:     // is deliberately narrower than V70's direct-drain experiment:

007890:     // - it never acquires Gate from the Vulkan presenter/completion thread;

007979:     private static void RunResumableDcbDrainWorker(SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007980:     {
007981:         while (true)
007982:         {

007987:                 {
007988:                     DrainResumableDcbs(drainContext, gpuState, tracePackets: _traceAgc);

007993:             {
007994:                 continue;
007995:             }

007999:                 Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref gpuState.DrainWorkerActive, 1, 0) != 0)

008024:     // upstream 0.0.3 already uses in ParseSubmittedDcb. Producer-intent lookahead
008025:     // remains enabled, but its PM4 reads now hit a thread-local byte window
008026:     // instead of taking the guest-memory reader path once per dword.

008036:     private static void PreindexSubmittedProducerIntentsCore(
008037:         CpuContext ctx,
008038:         SubmittedDcbState state,
008039:         ulong commandAddress,
008040:         uint dwordCount,
008041:         ulong submissionId,
008042:         HashSet<(ulong Address, uint Dwords)> visited,
008043:         int depth)
008044:     {
008045:         if (commandAddress == 0 ||
008046:             dwordCount == 0 ||
008047:             dwordCount > 1_000_000 ||
008048:             depth > MaxSubmittedChainDepth ||
008049:             !visited.Add((commandAddress, dwordCount)))
008050:         {
008051:             return;
008052:         }
008053: 
008054:         var byteCount = checked((int)(dwordCount * sizeof(uint)));
008055:         var rented = GuestDataPool.Shared.Rent(byteCount);
008056: 
008057:         // Preindex can recurse through INDIRECT_BUFFER. Preserve the parent
008058:         // thread-local DCB window while a child chain gets its own snapshot.
008059:         var previousBuffer = _dcbWindowBuffer;
008060:         var previousStart = _dcbWindowStart;
008061:         var previousLength = _dcbWindowByteLength;
008062:         var bulkLoaded = false;
008063: 
008064:         try
008065:         {
008066:             _dcbWindowBuffer = null;
008067:             _dcbWindowStart = 0;
008068:             _dcbWindowByteLength = 0;
008069: 
008070:             if (ctx.Memory.TryRead(commandAddress, rented.AsSpan(0, byteCount)))
008071:             {
008072:                 _dcbWindowBuffer = rented;
008073:                 _dcbWindowStart = commandAddress;
008074:                 _dcbWindowByteLength = byteCount;
008075:                 bulkLoaded = true;
008076:             }
008077:             else
008078:             {
008079:                 Interlocked.Increment(ref _pm4PreindexBulkFallbackCountV74062);
008080:             }
008081: 
008082:             var scanCount =
008083:                 Interlocked.Increment(ref _pm4PreindexBulkScanCountV74062);
008084:             var totalDwords =
008085:                 Interlocked.Add(ref _pm4PreindexBulkDwordsV74062, dwordCount);
008086: 
008087:             if (_tracePm4PreindexBulkV74062 &&
008088:                 (scanCount <= 128 || (scanCount & (scanCount - 1)) == 0))
008089:             {
008090:                 Console.Error.WriteLine(
008091:                     $"[V74.0.62][PM4_PREINDEX_BULK] count={scanCount} " +
008092:                     $"addr=0x{commandAddress:X16} dwords={dwordCount} " +
008093:                     $"total_dwords={totalDwords} bulk={(bulkLoaded ? 1 : 0)} " +
008094:                     $"depth={depth} queue={state.QueueName} " +
008095:                     $"submission={submissionId}");
008096:             }
008097: 
008098:             PreindexSubmittedProducerIntentsCoreWindowedV74062(
008099:                 ctx,
008100:                 state,
008101:                 commandAddress,
008102:                 dwordCount,
008103:                 submissionId,
008104:                 visited,
008105:                 depth);
008106:         }
008107:         finally
008108:         {
008109:             _dcbWindowBuffer = previousBuffer;
008110:             _dcbWindowStart = previousStart;
008111:             _dcbWindowByteLength = previousLength;
008112:             GuestDataPool.Shared.Return(rented);

008114:     }
008115: 
008116:     private static void PreindexSubmittedProducerIntentsCoreWindowedV74062(
008117:         CpuContext ctx,
008118:         SubmittedDcbState state,
008119:         ulong commandAddress,
008120:         uint dwordCount,
008121:         ulong submissionId,
008122:         HashSet<(ulong Address, uint Dwords)> visited,
008123:         int depth)
008124:     {
008125:         var offset = 0u;
008126:         while (offset < dwordCount)
008127:         {
008128:             var packetAddress = commandAddress + ((ulong)offset * sizeof(uint));
008129:             if (!TryReadUInt32(ctx, packetAddress, out var header))
008130:             {
008131:                 return;
008132:             }
008133: 
008134:             var packetType = header >> 30;
008135:             if (packetType == 2)
008136:             {
008137:                 offset++;
008138:                 continue;
008139:             }
008140: 
008141:             if (packetType != 3)
008142:             {
008143:                 return;
008144:             }
008145: 
008146:             var length = Pm4Length(header);
008147:             if (length == 0 || offset + length > dwordCount)
008148:             {
008149:                 return;
008150:             }
008151: 
008152:             var op = (header >> 8) & 0xFFu;
008153:             var register = (header >> 2) & 0x3Fu;
008154: 
008155:             if (TryDescribeSubmittedProducer(
008156:                     ctx,
008157:                     packetAddress,
008158:                     length,
008159:                     op,
008160:                     register,
008161:                     out var producerAddress,
008162:                     out var producerLength,
008163:                     out var producerName))
008164:             {
008165:                 RegisterPlannedLabelProducer(
008166:                     ctx.Memory,
008167:                     state.QueueName,
008168:                     submissionId,
008169:                     packetAddress,
008170:                     producerAddress,
008171:                     producerLength,
008172:                     producerName);
008173:             }
008174: 
008175:             if (op == ItIndirectBuffer &&
008176:                 length >= 4 &&
008177:                 TryReadUInt32(ctx, packetAddress + 4, out var chainLow) &&
008178:                 TryReadUInt32(ctx, packetAddress + 8, out var chainHigh) &&
008179:                 TryReadUInt32(ctx, packetAddress + 12, out var chainDwordsRaw))
008180:             {
008181:                 var chainAddress = ((ulong)(chainHigh & 0xFFFFu) << 32) | chainLow;
008182:                 var chainDwords = chainDwordsRaw & 0xFFFFFu;
008183:                 if (chainAddress != 0 && chainDwords != 0)
008184:                 {
008185:                     PreindexSubmittedProducerIntentsCore(
008186:                         ctx,
008187:                         state,
008188:                         chainAddress,
008189:                         chainDwords,
008190:                         submissionId,
008191:                         visited,
008192:                         depth + 1);
008193:                 }
008194:             }
008195: 
008196:             offset += length;
008197:         }
008198:     }

007527:         void ApplyAndQueueCompletion()
007528:         {
007529:             action();
007530:             // No label producer â†’ nothing to wake; skip the follow-up enqueue
007531:             // that was doubling OrderedGuestAction traffic during load.
007532:             if (producer is null)
007533:             {
007534:                 CompleteAndWake();
007535:                 return;
007536:             }
007537: 
007538:             // Release/write-data paths cannot enqueue Vulkan image mirrors, so
007539:             // complete the producer in the same ordered action. DMA can enqueue
007540:             // a mirror while applying; defer completion until after those
007541:             // follow-ups so waiters see the mirrored image.
007542:             if (!deferLabelCompletion)
007543:             {
007544:                 CompleteAndWake();
007545:                 return;
007546:             }
007547: 
007548:             if (GuestGpu.Current.SubmitOrderedGuestAction(
007549:                     CompleteAndWake,
007550:                     $"{debugName} completion") == 0)
007551:             {
007552:                 CompleteAndWake();
007553:             }
007554:         }

007556:         // SHARPEMU_V74_0_56_18_CROSS_QUEUE_WATCHED_INLINE_WRITE
007557:         // WRITE_DATA is an immediate command-processor memory packet; the PS5
007558:         // completion primitive is RELEASE_MEM/EOP. If another logical queue is
007559:         // already suspended on this exact label, executing this CPU-resident
007560:         // payload now avoids a host-fence/ordered-action round trip while still
007561:         // leaving every same-queue dependency on the conservative path.
007562:         var crossQueueWaiterCountV7405618 =
007563:             _crossQueueWatchedInlineWriteV7405618 &&

007573:                     producerAddress,

007575:                     state.QueueName)

007738:     private static void RequestResumableDcbDrain(CpuContext ctx, SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007739:     {
007740:         Volatile.Write(ref gpuState.PendingDrainContext, ctx);
007741:         Interlocked.Exchange(ref gpuState.DrainPending, 1);
007742: 
007743:         if (_dedicatedWaitDrainV74071 &&
007744:             EnsureDedicatedResumableDcbDrainWorkerV74071(gpuState))
007745:         {
007746:             gpuState.DedicatedDrainSignal.Set();
007747:             return;
007748:         }
007749: 
007750:         QueueLegacyResumableDcbDrainWorkerV74071(gpuState);
007751:     }
007752: 
007753:     private static void QueueLegacyResumableDcbDrainWorkerV74071(
007754:         SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007755:     {
007756:         if (Interlocked.CompareExchange(ref gpuState.DrainWorkerActive, 1, 0) == 0)
007757:         {
007758:             ThreadPool.UnsafeQueueUserWorkItem(
007759:                 static state => RunResumableDcbDrainWorker(state),
007760:                 gpuState,
007761:                 preferLocal: false);
007762:         }
007763:     }
007764: 
007765:     private static bool EnsureDedicatedResumableDcbDrainWorkerV74071(
007766:         SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007767:     {
007768:         if (Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainWorkerStarted) != 0)
007769:         {
007770:             return true;
007771:         }
007772: 
007773:         if (Interlocked.CompareExchange(
007774:                 ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainWorkerStarted,
007775:                 1,
007776:                 0) != 0)
007777:         {
007778:             return true;
007779:         }
007780: 
007781:         try
007782:         {
007783:             var worker = new System.Threading.Thread(
007784:                 RunDedicatedResumableDcbDrainWorkerV74071)
007785:             {
007786:                 IsBackground = true,
007787:                 Name = "SharpEmu-AGC-WaitDrain",
007788:             };
007789: 
007790:             gpuState.DedicatedDrainThread = worker;
007791:             worker.Start(gpuState);
007792: 
007793:             Console.Error.WriteLine(
007794:                 "[V74.0.71][DEDICATED_WAIT_DRAIN] worker_started=1");
007795:             return true;
007796:         }
007797:         catch (Exception ex)
007798:         {
007799:             gpuState.DedicatedDrainThread = null;
007800:             Volatile.Write(ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainWorkerStarted, 0);
007801:             Console.Error.WriteLine(
007802:                 $"[V74.0.71][DEDICATED_WAIT_DRAIN] worker_start_failed " +
007803:                 $"type={ex.GetType().Name}; falling back to ThreadPool");
007804:             return false;
007805:         }
007806:     }
007807: 
007808:     private static void RunDedicatedResumableDcbDrainWorkerV74071(
007809:         object? state)
007810:     {
007811:         if (state is not SubmittedGpuState gpuState)
007812:         {
007813:             return;
007814:         }
007815: 
007816:         while (true)
007817:         {
007818:             gpuState.DedicatedDrainSignal.WaitOne();
007819: 
007820:             while (true)
007821:             {
007822:                 Interlocked.Exchange(ref gpuState.DrainPending, 0);
007823:                 var drainContext =
007824:                     Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.PendingDrainContext);
007825: 
007826:                 var resumed = 0;
007827:                 var gateWaitMilliseconds = 0.0;
007828: 
007829:                 if (drainContext is not null)
007830:                 {
007831:                     var gateWaitStart =
007832:                         System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
007833: 
007834:                     lock (gpuState.Gate)
007835:                     {
007836:                         gateWaitMilliseconds =
007837:                             (System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() -
007838:                              gateWaitStart) *
007839:                             1000.0 /
007840:                             System.Diagnostics.Stopwatch.Frequency;
007841: 
007842:                         resumed = DrainResumableDcbs(
007843:                             drainContext,
007844:                             gpuState,
007845:                             tracePackets: _traceAgc);
007846:                     }
007847: 
007848:                     var traceCount = Interlocked.Increment(
007849:                         ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainTraceCount);
007850:                     var slowGate = gateWaitMilliseconds >= 5.0;
007851:                     if (slowGate)
007852:                     {
007853:                         Interlocked.Increment(
007854:                             ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainSlowGateCount);
007855:                     }
007856: 
007857:                     if (resumed > 0 ||
007858:                         slowGate ||
007859:                         traceCount <= 128 ||
007860:                         (traceCount & (traceCount - 1)) == 0)
007861:                     {
007862:                         Console.Error.WriteLine(
007863:                             $"[V74.0.71][DEDICATED_WAIT_DRAIN] " +
007864:                             $"count={traceCount} resumed={resumed} " +
007865:                             $"gate_wait_ms={gateWaitMilliseconds:F3} " +
007866:                             $"pending={Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending)} " +
007867:                             $"slow_gate={Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DedicatedDrainSlowGateCount)}");
007868:                     }
007869:                 }
007870: 
007871:                 // Producer completion can race with the drain above. If it
007872:                 // marked another pass pending, consume it immediately on this
007873:                 // same dedicated worker rather than scheduling a new host job.
007874:                 if (Volatile.Read(ref gpuState.DrainPending) != 0)
007875:                 {
007876:                     continue;
007877:                 }
007878: 
007879:                 break;
007880:             }
007881:         }
007882:     }

007884:     // V74.0.72: V71 proved the dominant remaining producer->waiter latency is
007885:     // contention on SubmittedGpuState.Gate, not ThreadPool startup. This helper
007886:     // is deliberately narrower than V70's direct-drain experiment:
007887:     //
007888:     // - it runs only on a thread that ALREADY owns gpuState.Gate;
007889:     // - it is called only between fully parsed PM4 packets;
007890:     // - it never acquires Gate from the Vulkan presenter/completion thread;
007891:     // - it preserves/restores the outer DCB snapshot because a resumed queue
007892:     //   can recursively enter ParseSubmittedDcb;
007893:     // - GateOwnerDrainActive prevents recursive drain-on-drain.

005759:     private static void NotifySubmittedDcbCompleted(
005760:         SubmittedGpuState gpuState,
005761:         SubmittedDcbState state,
005762:         ulong submissionId)

005765:         {

005767:         }

005770:         // Hardware raises an end-of-pipe interrupt for every submission on every
005771:         // queue, so this is unconditional. It stays safe for titles that do not
005772:         // want it because delivery is registration-gated: TriggerRegisteredEvents
005773:         // only queues onto equeues that registered this exact ident through
005774:         // sceAgcDriverAddEqEvent. Graphics keeps ident 0; a compute queue uses the
005775:         // owner handle it was submitted under.
005776:         var completionEventId = state.CompletionEventId;

005779:         void TriggerCompletionEvents()
005780:         {

005782:                 completionEventId,
005783:                 KernelEventQueueCompatExports.KernelEventFilterGraphics,
005784:                 completionEventId);
005785:             // The broad fan-out wakes graphics registrations whose ident never
005786:             // matches anything the driver publishes. That is a compatibility
005787:             // guess rather than hardware behavior, so it stays opt-in and stays
005788:             // on the graphics queue where it was measured.
005789:             if (isGraphics && _compatibilitySubmitCompletionEvent)
005790:             {

005798:

005800:         // ordered guest-memory writes have finished. Put the notification on that
005801:         // same logical queue instead of approximating completion with a timer or a

022081:         // Returns the source row length in texels when the upload is a linear
022082:         // image whose rows are padded to a wider hardware pitch, or 0 when the
022083:         // data is an exact tightly packed match or not a recognisable padded
022084:         // layout (in which case the caller keeps rejecting it). Only a pitch
022085:         // equal to the width rounded up to a common alignment is accepted, so an
022086:         // oversized buffer that still carries mip data is left rejected rather
022087:         // than mis-copied.
022088:         private bool TryResolveGuestImageCopyResource(
022089:             ulong address,
022090:             uint width,
022091:             uint height,
022092:             bool preferFreshest,
022093:             out GuestImageResource resource)
022094:         {
022095:             GuestImageResource? best = null;
022096: 
022097:             void Consider(GuestImageResource candidate)
022098:             {
022099:                 var logicalMatch =
022100:                     candidate.LogicalWidth == width &&
022101:                     candidate.LogicalHeight == height;
022102:                 var physicalMatch =
022103:                     candidate.Width == width &&
022104:                     candidate.Height == height;
022105:                 if (!logicalMatch && !physicalMatch)
022106:                 {
022107:                     return;
022108:                 }
022109: 
022110:                 if (best is null ||
022111:                     preferFreshest &&
022112:                     candidate.ContentGeneration > best.ContentGeneration)
022113:                 {
022114:                     best = candidate;
022115:                 }
022116:             }
022117: 
022118:             if (_guestImages.TryGetValue(address, out var active))
022119:             {
022120:                 Consider(active);
022121:             }
022122: 
022123:             foreach (var (key, candidate) in _guestImageVariants)
022124:             {
022125:                 if (key.Address == address)
022126:                 {
022127:                     Consider(candidate);
022128:                 }
022129:             }
022130: 
022131:             resource = best!;
022132:             return best is not null;
022133:         }

008345:         // Translated draws are recorded into a shared command buffer and
008346:         // submitted once per drained work batch: on MoltenVK every

008348:         // to be paid per draw and dominated the frame time.

008355:         // Consecutive draws into the same target stay inside one render pass:
008356:         // on MoltenVK every render pass is a Metal render encoder, and one
008357:         // encoder per draw was the dominant per-draw fixed cost after submit
008358:         // batching. The pass closes when the target changes, when a draw
008359:         // needs transfer/storage work outside a pass, or when the batch
008360:         // flushes.

008419:         {

008423:             }

008441:                     batchWorkV7405620 > 0)

008444:                         Interlocked.Increment(
008445:                             ref _v7405620BatchSubmitCount);

008447:                         Interlocked.Add(
008448:                             ref _v7405620BatchSubmittedWork,
008449:                             batchWorkV7405620);
008450: 
008451:                     if (submitCountV7405620 <= 128 ||
008452:                         (submitCountV7405620 &
008453:                          (submitCountV7405620 - 1)) == 0)

008461:                             $"queue={_activeGuestQueue.Name} " +
008462:                             $"submission={_activeGuestQueue.SubmissionId}");
008463:                     }
008464:                 }
008465:             }
008466:             catch
008467:             {
008468:                 // The batch never reached the queue: release everything it
008469:                 // owned here so the stale lists cannot ride into the next
008470:                 // batch's submission.

008472:                 {
008473:                     DestroyTranslatedDrawResources(resources);
008474:                 }
008475:

008477:                 {
008478:                     _vk.DestroyBuffer(_device, buffer, null);
008479:                     _vk.FreeMemory(_device, memory, null);
008480:                 }
008481: 
008482:                 foreach (var transients in _batchRetireDetile)
008483:                 {
008484:                     _detilePass?.Retire(transients);
008485:                 }
008486: 
008487:                 ReleaseGuestCommandBuffer(_batchCommandBuffer);
008488:                 throw;
008489:             }
008490:             finally
008491:             {
008492:                 _batchResources.Clear();
008493:                 _batchTraceImages.Clear();
008494:                 _batchRetireBuffers.Clear();
008495:                 _batchRetireDetile.Clear();

008497:             }

008646:         private void EnsureGuestSubmissionCapacity()
008647:         {
008648:             CollectCompletedGuestSubmissions(waitForOldest: false);
008649:             if (_pendingGuestSubmissions.Count < MaxInFlightGuestSubmissions)
008650:             {
008651:                 return;
008652:             }
008653: 
008654:             if (_kytyComputeSubmissionFairnessV74033)
008655:             {
008656:                 // RenderCore stops taking new guest work at the normal target.
008657:                 // Reaching this method at/above the target therefore means the
008658:                 // *current* guest item needed more than one host submission
008659:                 // (resource init/upload, etc.). Permit bounded headroom instead
008660:                 // of sleeping the presentation thread behind the oldest fence.
008661:                 if (_pendingGuestSubmissions.Count <
008662:                     SoftEmergencyGuestSubmissionLimitV74033)
008663:                 {
008664:                     var overcommitCount = Interlocked.Increment(
008665:                         ref _v74033CapacityOvercommitTraceCount);
008666:                     if (overcommitCount <= 64 ||
008667:                         (overcommitCount & (overcommitCount - 1)) == 0)
008668:                     {
008669:                         Console.Error.WriteLine(
008670:                             $"[V74.0.33][SUBMISSION_OVERCOMMIT] " +
008671:                             $"count={overcommitCount} pending={_pendingGuestSubmissions.Count} " +
008672:                             $"target={MaxInFlightGuestSubmissions} " +
008673:                             $"emergency={SoftEmergencyGuestSubmissionLimitV74033}");
008674:                     }
008675: 
008676:                     return;
008677:                 }
008678: 
008679:                 // Emergency safety only. A normal Demon's Souls compute item
008680:                 // should never reach this after unified submission is enabled.
008681:                 while (!_deviceLost &&
008682:                        _pendingGuestSubmissions.Count >=
008683:                            SoftEmergencyGuestSubmissionLimitV74033)
008684:                 {
008685:                     CollectCompletedGuestSubmissions(waitForOldest: true);
008686:                     CollectAbandonedGuestSubmissions();
008687:                 }
008688: 
008689:                 return;
008690:             }
008691: 
008692:             if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS())
008693:             {
008694:                 // Keep the bounded probe on Cocoa's event-pump thread.
008695:                 CollectCompletedGuestSubmissions(
008696:                     waitForOldest: true,
008697:                     maxWaitNs: _submissionCapacityWaitNs == 0
008698:                         ? _guestFenceWaitTimeoutNs
008699:                         : _submissionCapacityWaitNs);
008700:                 return;
008701:             }
008702: 
008703:             // Legacy Windows/Linux hard-cap behavior.
008704:             while (!_deviceLost &&
008705:                    _pendingGuestSubmissions.Count >= MaxInFlightGuestSubmissions)
008706:             {
008707:                 CollectCompletedGuestSubmissions(waitForOldest: true);
008708:                 CollectAbandonedGuestSubmissions();
008709:             }
008710:         }

008712:         private void WaitForAllGuestSubmissions()
008713:         {
008714:             while (_pendingGuestSubmissions.Count != 0)
008715:             {
008716:                 CollectCompletedGuestSubmissions(waitForOldest: true);
008717:             }
008718: 
008719:             while (_abandonedGuestSubmissions.Count != 0)
008720:             {
008721:                 CollectAbandonedGuestSubmissions();
008722:                 if (_abandonedGuestSubmissions.Count == 0)
008723:                 {
008724:                     break;
008725:                 }
008726: 
008727:                 if (!_abandonedGuestSubmissions.TryPeek(out var oldest))
008728:                 {
008729:                     break;
008730:                 }
008731: 
008732:                 var fence = oldest.Fence;
008733:                 var result = _vk.WaitForFences(
008734:                     _device,
008735:                     1,
008736:                     &fence,
008737:                     true,
008738:                     _guestFenceWaitTimeoutNs);
008739:                 if (result == Result.Timeout || result == Result.ErrorDeviceLost)
008740:                 {
008741:                     if (result == Result.ErrorDeviceLost)
008742:                     {
008743:                         _deviceLost = true;
008744:                     }
008745: 
008746:                     while (_abandonedGuestSubmissions.TryDequeue(out var abandoned))
008747:                     {
008748:                         RetireGuestSubmission(abandoned);
008749:                     }
008750: 
008751:                     break;
008752:                 }
008753: 
008754:                 Check(result, $"vkWaitForFences(abandoned: {oldest.DebugName})");
008755:                 CollectAbandonedGuestSubmissions();
008756:             }
008757:         }

008759:         private void CollectCompletedGuestSubmissions(bool waitForOldest, ulong maxWaitNs = 0)
008760:         {
008761:             var retiredAnyV7405621 = false;
008762: 
008763:             if (waitForOldest && _pendingGuestSubmissions.TryPeek(out var oldest))
008764:             {
008765:                 var fence = oldest.Fence;
008766:                 // maxWaitNs==0 => the full "is this submission hung" timeout,
008767:                 // which also emits the one-shot hang warning below. A shorter
008768:                 // capacity-probe wait (maxWaitNs>0) must NOT report a hang: the
008769:                 // submission is still tracked and will be collected once the GPU
008770:                 // finishes it on a later frame.
008771:                 var isProbeWait = maxWaitNs != 0 && maxWaitNs < _guestFenceWaitTimeoutNs;
008772:                 var waitNs = maxWaitNs != 0 ? maxWaitNs : _guestFenceWaitTimeoutNs;
008773:                 var result = _vk.WaitForFences(
008774:                     _device,
008775:                     1,
008776:                     &fence,
008777:                     true,
008778:                     waitNs);
008779:                 if (result == Result.Timeout)
008780:                 {
008781:                     // A GPU submission whose fence never signals (typically a
008782:                     // mistranslated compute shader that hangs the Metal queue)
008783:                     // would otherwise block the render thread forever, starving
008784:                     // the swapchain present (black screen). Log the culprit and
008785:                     // continue so at least the last good frame can be shown.
008786:                     if (isProbeWait)
008787:                     {
008788:                         return;
008789:                     }
008790: 
008791:                     if (_tracedFenceTimeouts.Add(oldest.DebugName))
008792:                     {
008793:                         Console.Error.WriteLine(
008794:                             $"[LOADER][WARN] vk.fence_wait_timeout submission='{oldest.DebugName}' " +
008795:                             $"queue={oldest.Queue.Name} timeline={oldest.Timeline} " +
008796:                             $"age_ms={((Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() - oldest.SubmittedTimestampV74049) * 1000.0 / Stopwatch.Frequency):F3} " +
008797:                             $"Ã¢â‚¬â€ GPU work not completing after {_guestFenceWaitTimeoutNs / 1_000_000}ms; " +
008798:                             "abandoning in-flight tracking so later frames are not re-blocked.");
008799:                     }
008800: 
008801:                     // Move out of the blocking queue without destroying GPU
008802:                     // objects yet Ã¢â‚¬â€ the work may still be running. Poll and
008803:                     // retire from the abandoned list once the fence signals.
008804:                     _pendingGuestSubmissions.Dequeue();
008805:                     _abandonedGuestSubmissions.Enqueue(oldest);
008806:                 }
008807:                 else if (result == Result.ErrorDeviceLost)
008808:                 {
008809:                     _deviceLost = true;
008810:                     if (!_deviceLostLogged)
008811:                     {
008812:                         _deviceLostLogged = true;
008813:                         var work = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastGuestWorkLabel)
008814:                             ? $"last_work={_lastGuestWorkLabel}"
008815:                             : "work=<none>";
008816:                         Console.Error.WriteLine(
008817:                             "[LOADER][ERROR] Vulkan device lost; dropping subsequent guest GPU work. " +
008818:                             $"{work} last_submit={oldest.DebugName} " +
008819:                             "vkWaitForFences(guest) failed with ErrorDeviceLost.");
008820:                     }
008821:                 }
008822:                 else
008823:                 {
008824:                     Check(result, $"vkWaitForFences(guest: {oldest.DebugName})");
008825:                 }
008826:             }
008827: 
008828:             while (_pendingGuestSubmissions.TryPeek(out var submission))
008829:             {
008830:                 var status = _vk.GetFenceStatus(_device, submission.Fence);
008831:                 if (status == Result.NotReady && !_deviceLost)
008832:                 {
008833:                     break;
008834:                 }
008835: 
008836:                 if (status == Result.ErrorDeviceLost)
008837:                 {
008838:                     // Pending fences never signal on a lost device; retire the
008839:                     // submission anyway so teardown and back-pressure survive.
008840:                     _deviceLost = true;
008841:                 }
008842:                 else if (status != Result.NotReady)
008843:                 {
008844:                     Check(status, $"vkGetFenceStatus(guest: {submission.DebugName})");
008845:                 }
008846: 
008847:                 _pendingGuestSubmissions.Dequeue();
008848:                 RetireGuestSubmission(submission);
008849:                 retiredAnyV7405621 = true;
008850:             }
008851: 
008852:             // [V74.0.56.21][GPU_MAINTENANCE_THROTTLE]
008853:             // Always retire a signalled real submission immediately. Expensive
008854:             // cache/deferred-resource maintenance is different: if this call
008855:             // merely observed the oldest fence as NotReady, repeating every
008856:             // trim/scan hundreds of times per second is host CPU waste.
008857:             var nowV7405621 = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
008858:             var runMaintenanceV7405621 =
008859:                 !_gpuMaintenanceThrottleV7405621 ||
008860:                 retiredAnyV7405621 ||
008861:                 _deviceLost ||
008862:                 nowV7405621 >=
008863:                     Volatile.Read(
008864:                         ref _nextGpuMaintenanceTickV7405621);
008865: 
008866:             if (runMaintenanceV7405621)
008867:             {
008868:                 Volatile.Write(
008869:                     ref _nextGpuMaintenanceTickV7405621,

008871:                     _gpuMaintenanceIntervalTicksV7405621);

010106:                 returned.Add(indexBuffer.Data))
010107:             {

023832:         private void ReinterpretGuestImageFormat(
023833:             GuestImageResource resource,
023834:             Format format,
023835:             bool promoteRenderPass,
023836:             GuestRenderTarget target)
023837:         {
023838:             if (_realFormatConversionEnabled &&
023839:                 RequiresRealFormatConversion(resource.Format, format))
023840:             {
023841:                 ConvertGuestImageBytesInPlace(resource, resource.Format, format);
023842:                 foreach (var cachedEntry in resource.ReinterpretCache.Values)
023843:                 {
023844:                     DestroyReinterpretedGuestImageViews(cachedEntry);
023845:                 }
023846:                 resource.ReinterpretCache.Clear();
023847:             }
023848: 
023849:             var previous = new ReinterpretedGuestImageViews(
023850:                 resource.View,
023851:                 resource.MipViews,
023852:                 resource.RenderPass,
023853:                 resource.InitialRenderPass,
023854:                 resource.Framebuffer);
023855:             resource.ReinterpretCache.TryAdd(resource.Format, previous);
023856: 
023857:             if (resource.ReinterpretCache.Remove(format, out var cached))
023858:             {
023859:                 resource.View = cached.View;
023860:                 resource.MipViews = cached.MipViews;
023861:                 resource.RenderPass = cached.RenderPass;
023862:                 resource.InitialRenderPass = cached.InitialRenderPass;
023863:                 resource.Framebuffer = cached.Framebuffer;
023864:                 resource.Format = format;
023865:                 return;
023866:             }
023867: 
023868:             var viewInfo = new ImageViewCreateInfo
023869:             {
023870:                 SType = StructureType.ImageViewCreateInfo,
023871:                 Image = resource.Image,
023872:                 ViewType = ImageViewType.Type2D,
023873:                 Format = format,
023874:                 Components = new ComponentMapping(
023875:                     ComponentSwizzle.Identity,
023876:                     ComponentSwizzle.Identity,
023877:                     ComponentSwizzle.Identity,
023878:                     ComponentSwizzle.Identity),
023879:                 SubresourceRange = ColorSubresourceRange(0, resource.MipLevels),
023880:             };
023881:             Check(
023882:                 _vk.CreateImageView(_device, &viewInfo, null, out var newView),
023883:                 "vkCreateImageView(guest reinterpret)");
023884:             resource.View = newView;
023885: 
023886:             var mipViews = new ImageView[resource.MipLevels];
023887:             for (uint mipLevel = 0; mipLevel < resource.MipLevels; mipLevel++)
023888:             {
023889:                 viewInfo.SubresourceRange = ColorSubresourceRange(mipLevel, 1);
023890:                 Check(
023891:                     _vk.CreateImageView(_device, &viewInfo, null, out var mipView),
023892:                     "vkCreateImageView(guest reinterpret mip)");
023893:                 mipViews[mipLevel] = mipView;
023894:             }
023895: 
023896:             resource.MipViews = mipViews;
023897:             resource.Format = format;
023898: 
023899:             if (promoteRenderPass)
023900:             {
023901:                 var attachmentView = resource.MipViews.Length > 0
023902:                     ? resource.MipViews[0]
023903:                     : resource.View;
023904:                 var promoted = CreateRenderPassAndFramebuffer(
023905:                     resource.Format,
023906:                     attachmentView,
023907:                     resource.Width,
023908:                     resource.Height);
023909:                 resource.RenderPass = promoted.RenderPass;
023910:                 resource.InitialRenderPass = promoted.InitialRenderPass;
023911:                 resource.Framebuffer = promoted.Framebuffer;
023912:                 var promotedName = GuestImageDebugName(target, format);
023913:                 SetDebugName(ObjectType.RenderPass, promoted.RenderPass.Handle, $"{promotedName} renderpass");
023914:                 SetDebugName(ObjectType.RenderPass, promoted.InitialRenderPass.Handle, $"{promotedName} initial-renderpass");
023915:                 SetDebugName(ObjectType.Framebuffer, promoted.Framebuffer.Handle, $"{promotedName} framebuffer");
023916:             }

023950:         private void DestroyGuestImage(GuestImageResource resource)
023951:         {
023952:             foreach (var depthFramebuffer in resource.DepthFramebuffers.Values)
023953:             {
023954:                 DestroyDepthFramebuffer(depthFramebuffer);
023955:             }
023956:             resource.DepthFramebuffers.Clear();

023959:             {
023960:                 if (view.Handle != 0)

023962:                     _vk.DestroyImageView(_device, view, null);
023963:                 }
023964:             }
023965:             resource.FormatViews.Clear();
023966: 
023967:             foreach (var cached in resource.ReinterpretCache.Values)
023968:             {
023969:                 DestroyReinterpretedGuestImageViews(cached);
023970:             }
023971:             resource.ReinterpretCache.Clear();
023972: 
023973:             if (resource.Framebuffer.Handle != 0)
023974:             {
023975:                 _vk.DestroyFramebuffer(_device, resource.Framebuffer, null);
023976:             }
023977: 
023978:             if (resource.RenderPass.Handle != 0)
023979:             {
023980:                 _vk.DestroyRenderPass(_device, resource.RenderPass, null);
023981:             }
023982: 
023983:             if (resource.InitialRenderPass.Handle != 0)
023984:             {
023985:                 _vk.DestroyRenderPass(_device, resource.InitialRenderPass, null);
023986:             }
023987: 
023988:             if (resource.View.Handle != 0)
023989:             {
023990:                 _vk.DestroyImageView(_device, resource.View, null);
